    PROCEDURE Authorize@50();
    VAR
      TransactionLogEntry@1001 : Record 829;
    BEGIN
      IF NOT PaymentMgt.IsValidPaymentMethod("Payment Method Code") THEN
        ERROR(Text069,FIELDCAPTION("Payment Method Code"));
      PaymentTransLogMgt.FindValidAuthorizationEntry("Document Type","No.",TransactionLogEntry);
      IF TransactionLogEntry."Entry No." = PaymentMgt.AuthorizeSalesDoc(Rec,0,TRUE) THEN
        ERROR(Text067,TransactionLogEntry."Document Type",TransactionLogEntry.Amount,TransactionLogEntry."Transaction Date-Time",
          TransactionLogEntry."Document No.");
      "Authorization Required" := TRUE;
      MODIFY;
    END;

    PROCEDURE Void@51();
    VAR
      TransactionLogEntry@1001 : Record 829;
      TransactionLogMgt@1002 : Codeunit 829;
    BEGIN
      IF NOT PaymentMgt.IsValidPaymentMethod(Rec."Payment Method Code") THEN
        ERROR(Text069,FIELDCAPTION("Payment Method Code"));
      CLEAR(PaymentMgt);
      PaymentMgt.CheckSalesDoc(Rec);
      IF TransactionLogMgt.FindValidAuthorizationEntry("Document Type","No.",TransactionLogEntry) THEN
        PaymentMgt.VoidSalesDoc(Rec,TransactionLogEntry)
      ELSE
        MESSAGE(Text068);
      "Authorization Required" := FALSE;
      MODIFY;
    END;

    END;

    PROCEDURE SynchronizeForReservations@42(VAR NewSalesLine@1000 : Record 37;OldSalesLine@1001 : Record 37);
    BEGIN
      NewSalesLine.CALCFIELDS("Reserved Quantity");
      IF NewSalesLine."Reserved Quantity" = 0 THEN
        EXIT;
      IF NewSalesLine."Location Code" <> OldSalesLine."Location Code" THEN
        NewSalesLine.VALIDATE("Location Code",OldSalesLine."Location Code");
      IF NewSalesLine."Bin Code" <> OldSalesLine."Bin Code" THEN
        NewSalesLine.VALIDATE("Bin Code",OldSalesLine."Bin Code");
      IF NewSalesLine.MODIFY THEN;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckADRExempt@8053001() R_IsExempt : Boolean;
    VAR
      L_recADR@8053000 : Record 8053054;
      L_recTransCategory@8053001 : Record 8053055;
      L_recSalesLine@8053002 : Record 37;
      L_recItem@8053004 : Record 27;
      L_TotalADR@8053003 : Decimal;
      L_MaxiWeight@8053005 : Decimal;
      L_TotalWeight@8053006 : Decimal;
      L_LastTransCategory@8053007 : Code[10];
      L_IsMultipleCategory@8053008 : Boolean;
      WarehouseShipHeader_L@8053009 : Record 7320;
      Index_L@8053010 : Integer;
      NbCat_L@8053011 : Integer;
      UniqueCategory_L@8053012 : Integer;
      ShipCategory_L@8053013 : Record 8053055;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04

      R_IsExempt := TRUE;

      FOR Index_L := 1 TO 4 DO BEGIN
        ShipCategory_G[Index_L] := '';
        RealQtyShipPerCategory_G[Index_L] := 0;
        CalcQtyShipPerCategory_G[Index_L] := 0;
        MaxWeightPerCategory_G[Index_L] := 0;
      END;

      Index_L := 1;
      ShipCategory_L.RESET;
      ShipCategory_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Transport Category");
      ShipCategory_L.SETFILTER("Transport Category",'<>%1','0');
      IF ShipCategory_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          ShipCategory_G[Index_L] := ShipCategory_L."Transport Category";
          Index_L := Index_L + 1;
        UNTIL (ShipCategory_L.NEXT = 0) OR (Index_L > 4);

      L_recSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
      L_recSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
      L_recSalesLine.SETRANGE(Type,L_recSalesLine.Type :: Item);
      L_recSalesLine.SETFILTER("Qty. to Ship",'<>%1',0);
      IF L_recSalesLine.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          WarehouseShipHeader_L.CalcDangerousMat(L_recSalesLine."No.",L_recSalesLine."Qty. to Ship",ShipCategory_G,
            RealQtyShipPerCategory_G,CalcQtyShipPerCategory_G,MaxWeightPerCategory_G,
            L_recSalesLine."Unit of Measure Code");
        UNTIL L_recSalesLine.NEXT = 0;

        Index_L := 1;
        NbCat_L := 0;
        TotalAdr_G := 0;
        WHILE (Index_L <= 4) DO BEGIN
          IF CalcQtyShipPerCategory_G[Index_L] <> 0 THEN BEGIN
            NbCat_L += 1;
            UniqueCategory_L := Index_L;
          END;
          TotalAdr_G += CalcQtyShipPerCategory_G[Index_L];
          Index_L += 1;
        END;

        R_IsExempt := TRUE;
        CASE NbCat_L OF
          0 : ;          // Pas de matiSres dangereuses
          1 :
            BEGIN        // Une seule matiSre dangereuse
              IF (RealQtyShipPerCategory_G[UniqueCategory_L] >=  MaxWeightPerCategory_G[UniqueCategory_L]) THEN
                R_IsExempt := FALSE;
            END;
          ELSE BEGIN     // Plusieurs matiSres dangereuses
            IF TotalAdr_G > 1000 THEN
              R_IsExempt := FALSE;
          END;
        END;

      END;

      EXIT(R_IsExempt);
      // <-- 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdatePostingDesc@60001();

    END;

    PROCEDURE GetNextLineNo@8053005(SHeader_P@8053000 : Record 36) : Integer;

    PROCEDURE ExtractSalesLine@8053000(SalesHeader_P@8053000 : Record 36);
    VAR
      SalesLine_L@8053001 : Record 37;
      SalesForTkOut_L@8053002 : Page 8053210;
      EntryNo_L@8053003 : Integer;
      SalesLineToInsert_L@8053004 : Record 37;
      LinkedQuantity_L@8053005 : Decimal;
      SalesLines_L@8053006 : Record 37;
      "<--3LI Text Constants-->"@8053008 : TextConst;
      Text8053000_L@8053007 : TextConst 'ENU=Order lines which contains item %1 %2 have been found for customer %3.Would you like to see them?;FRA=Des lignes de commandes contenant l''article %1 %2 ont 't' trouv'es pour le client %3.Voulez-vous les afficher?';
    BEGIN
      // --> 20111228-Log03-CMC02.01.00-BRA01
      IF (NOT SalesHeader_P.Adduction) OR (SalesHeader_P.Status<>SalesHeader_P.Status::Open) THEN
        ERROR(Text8053005);
      SalesLine_L.RESET;
      SalesLine_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Document Type",Type,"No.","Variant Code","Drop Shipment","Build Kit","Location Code","Shipment Date");
      SalesLine_L.SETRANGE(SalesLine_L."Document Type",SalesLine_L."Document Type"::Order);
      SalesLine_L.SETFILTER("Document No.",'<>%1',SalesHeader_P."No.");
      SalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Drop Shipment",FALSE);
      SalesLine_L.SETRANGE(Type,SalesLine_L.Type::Item);
      SalesLine_L.SETFILTER(SalesLine_L."Sell-to Customer No.","Sell-to Customer No.");
      SalesLine_L.SETFILTER(SalesLine_L."Outstanding Quantity",'<>0');
      IF SalesLine_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        SalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Status",Status::Released);
        SalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Linked Order No.",'');

        CLEAR(SalesForTkOut_L);
        SalesForTkOut_L.SETTABLEVIEW(SalesLine_L);
        SalesForTkOut_L.LOOKUPMODE := TRUE;
        IF SalesForTkOut_L.RUNMODAL=ACTION::LookupOK THEN BEGIN
          SalesForTkOut_L.GetSelection(SalesLine_L);
          IF SalesLine_L.FINDSET THEN
            REPEAT
              EntryNo_L := GetNextLineNo(SalesHeader_P);
              SalesLineToInsert_L.INIT;
              SalesLineToInsert_L.SetHeaderExtract(TRUE);
              SalesLineToInsert_L."Document Type" := SalesHeader_P."Document Type";
              SalesLineToInsert_L."Document No." := SalesHeader_P."No.";
              SalesLineToInsert_L."Line No." := EntryNo_L;
              SalesLineToInsert_L.SuspendStatusCheck(TRUE);
              SalesLineToInsert_L.Type := SalesLineToInsert_L.Type::Item;
              SalesLineToInsert_L.VALIDATE("No.",SalesLine_L."No.");
              SalesLineToInsert_L.VALIDATE("Variant Code",SalesLine_L."Variant Code");
              SalesLineToInsert_L.VALIDATE("Unit of Measure",SalesLine_L."Unit of Measure");
              SalesLineToInsert_L."Linked Order No." := SalesLine_L."Document No.";
              SalesLineToInsert_L."Linked Order Line No." := SalesLine_L."Line No.";
              IF SalesLineToInsert_L."Linked Order No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
                LinkedQuantity_L := 0;
                SalesLines_L.RESET;
                SalesLines_L.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type"::Order);
                SalesLines_L.SETRANGE(SalesLines_L.Type,SalesLines_L.Type::Item);
                SalesLines_L.SETRANGE("No.",SalesLineToInsert_L."No.");
                SalesLines_L.SETRANGE("Linked Order No.",SalesLineToInsert_L."Linked Order No.");
                IF (SalesLines_L.FINDSET) AND GUIALLOWED THEN
                  REPEAT
                    LinkedQuantity_L += SalesLines_L."Outstanding Quantity";
                  UNTIL SalesLines_L.NEXT = 0;
                SalesLineToInsert_L.VALIDATE("Linked Quantity",SalesLine_L."Outstanding Quantity");
                IF LinkedQuantity_L < SalesLines_L."Linked Quantity" THEN
                  SalesLineToInsert_L.VALIDATE("Linked Quantity",SalesLineToInsert_L."Linked Quantity"-LinkedQuantity_L);
                SalesLineToInsert_L.VALIDATE(Quantity,SalesLine_L."Outstanding Quantity"-LinkedQuantity_L);
                IF SalesLineToInsert_L.Quantity = 0 THEN BEGIN
                  SalesLineToInsert_L."Linked Order No." := '';
                  SalesLineToInsert_L."Linked Order Line No." := 0;
                  SalesLineToInsert_L."Linked Quantity" := 0;
                END;
              END;
              SalesLineToInsert_L.VALIDATE("Unit Price",SalesLine_L."Unit Price");
              SalesLineToInsert_L.VALIDATE("Line Discount %",SalesLine_L."Line Discount %");
              SalesLineToInsert_L."Negotiated Price":=TRUE;
              SalesLineToInsert_L."Cancel Remaining Qty":=TRUE;
              SalesLineToInsert_L."Price Anomaly":=SalesLine_L."Price Anomaly";
              SalesLineToInsert_L."Anomaly Approved" :=SalesLine_L."Anomaly Approved";
              SalesLineToInsert_L.INSERT(TRUE);
              SalesLineToInsert_L.CreateFreeProLineFromAdduction;
            UNTIL SalesLine_L.NEXT=0;
        END
      END ELSE
        MESSAGE(Text8053006);
      // <-- 20111228-Log03-CMC02.01.00-BRA01
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateVeternarianInfo@8053006();
    VAR
      Contact_L@8053000 : Record 5050;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20120511-Nut02-CMC02.01.00-CRE01

        "Vetenarian Adress 1" := '';
        "Vetenarian Adress 2" := '';
        "Vetenarian Post Code" := '';
        "Vetenarian City" := '';
        "Vetenarian County" := '';
        "Vetenarian Country Code" := '';

        "Vetenarian County" := Contact_L.County;
        "Vetenarian Country Code" := Contact_L."Country/Region Code";
      END;
      // <-- 20120511-Nut02-CMC02.01.00-CRE01
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetGroupAccount@8053007();
    VAR
      Cust_L@8053001 : Record 18;
      GroupAccCode_L@8053000 : Code[10];
    BEGIN
      // --> 20180524-WI00-ADV01.01.00-35245-CJY01
      //Retrait 32960, retour au code pr'c'dent
      {BEFORE
      // 20180207-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-ID32960-JLT01 -->
      {BEFORE
      // --> 20140311-FINA10-CMC02.02.00-1824-FEC01
      IF "Bill-to Customer No." = '' THEN
        EXIT;

      GroupAccCode_L := '';

      IF GUIALLOWED AND (NOT HideCollectifChoice_G) AND GroupAccountMgt_G.SalesIsGroupAccUpdateAllowed(Rec) THEN BEGIN
        GroupAccCode_L := GroupAccountMgt_G.LookupGroupAccount(0,"Bill-to Customer No.");
        IF GroupAccCode_L <> '' THEN
          VALIDATE("Group Account Code",GroupAccCode_L);
      END;

      Cust_L.GET("Bill-to Customer No.");
      IF (GroupAccCode_L = '') AND (Cust_L."Group Account Code" <> '') THEN
        VALIDATE("Group Account Code",Cust_L."Group Account Code");
      // <-- 20140311-FINA10-CMC02.02.00-1824-FEC01
      AFTER}


      IF NOT HideCollectifChoice_G THEN BEGIN
        IF ("Bill-to Customer No." > '') AND GUIALLOWED AND GroupAccountMgt_G.SalesIsGroupAccUpdateAllowed(Rec) THEN
          GroupAccCode_L := GroupAccountMgt_G.LookupGroupAccount(0,"Bill-to Customer No.");
        VALIDATE("Group Account Code",GroupAccCode_L);
      END;
      //<-- 20180207-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-ID32960-JLT01
      AFTER}

      IF "Bill-to Customer No." = '' THEN
        EXIT;

      GroupAccCode_L := '';

      IF GUIALLOWED AND (NOT HideCollectifChoice_G) AND GroupAccountMgt_G.SalesIsGroupAccUpdateAllowed(Rec) THEN BEGIN
        GroupAccCode_L := GroupAccountMgt_G.LookupGroupAccount(0,"Bill-to Customer No.");
        IF GroupAccCode_L <> '' THEN
          VALIDATE("Group Account Code",GroupAccCode_L);
      END;

      Cust_L.GET("Bill-to Customer No.");
      IF (GroupAccCode_L = '') AND (Cust_L."Group Account Code" <> '') THEN
        VALIDATE("Group Account Code",Cust_L."Group Account Code");
      // <-- 20180524-WI00-ADV01.01.00-35245-CJY01
    END;

    PROCEDURE LookUpVehicleCode@60002(VAR codeRegistrationVar_P@60000 : Code[20];codeCarrierCode_P@60001 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      recCMCVehicle_L@60002 : Record 8053049;
      recShippingAgent_L@60003 : Record 291;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20140718-APP16-TER01.01.00-ENS01
      IF recShippingAgent_L.GET(codeCarrierCode_P) AND
        (recShippingAgent_L."Check Transp./Immat. Trailers")
      THEN BEGIN
        recCMCVehicle_L.RESET;
        recCMCVehicle_L.FILTERGROUP := 2;
        recCMCVehicle_L.SETRANGE("Carrier Code",codeCarrierCode_P);
        recCMCVehicle_L.FILTERGROUP := 0;

        IF FORM.RUNMODAL(FORM::"CC77631 - T8053104",recCMCVehicle_L) = ACTION::LookupOK THEN
          codeRegistrationVar_P := recCMCVehicle_L.Registration;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        recCMCVehicle_L.RESET;

        IF FORM.RUNMODAL(FORM::"CC77631 - T8053104",recCMCVehicle_L) = ACTION::LookupOK THEN
          codeRegistrationVar_P := recCMCVehicle_L.Registration;
      END;
      // <-- 20140718-APP16-TER01.01.00-ENS01
    END;

    PROCEDURE ValidateVehicleCarrier@60003();
    VAR
      recCMCVehicle_L@60000 : Record 8053049;
      recShippingAgent_L@60001 : Record 291;
      Text000_L@60002 : TextConst 'ENU=%1 %2 does not match with the %3 %4.;FRA=%1 %2  ne correspond pas avec la %3 %4.';
    BEGIN
      // --> 20140731-APP16-TER01.01.00-ENS01
      IF recShippingAgent_L.GET("Shipping Agent Code") AND
        (recShippingAgent_L."Check Transp./Immat. Trailers")
      THEN BEGIN
        IF recCMCVehicle_L.GET("Vehicle Registration") THEN ;
        IF (recCMCVehicle_L."Carrier Code" <> "Shipping Agent Code") THEN
          ERROR(
            STRSUBSTNO(
              Text000_L,
                FIELDCAPTION("Vehicle Registration"),
                "Vehicle Registration",
                FIELDCAPTION("Shipping Agent Code"),
                "Shipping Agent Code"));
      END;
      // <-- 20140731-APP16-TER01.01.00-ENS01
    END;

    PROCEDURE RunSODistribution@60004();
    VAR
      SalesLines_L@60000 : Record 37;
      CustDocDistribuiton_L@60003 : Record 60004;
      CustRec_L@60007 : Record 18;
      CustDistMatrix_L@60004 : Page 60006;
      CreditLimitWarning_L@60008 : Page 343;
      Outsqty_L@60001 : Decimal;
      Totqty_L@60002 : Decimal;
      Distopt_L@60005 : Integer;
      BlockedCustTxt_L@60006 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // --> 20140903-APP21-TER01.01.00-PDC01
      TESTFIELD(Status,Status::Released);

      IF NOT "To Distribute" THEN
        ERROR(Text60001);

      IF ("Payment Discount %"<>0) OR
         ("Prepayment %"<>0) OR
         ("Prepmt. Payment Discount %"<>0) THEN
        ERROR(Text60002);

      Outsqty_L:=0;
      Totqty_L:=0;
      SalesLines_L.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
      SalesLines_L.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
      SalesLines_L.SETRANGE(Type,SalesLines_L.Type::Item);
      IF SalesLines_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          Outsqty_L+=SalesLines_L."Outstanding Quantity";
          Totqty_L+=SalesLines_L.Quantity;
        UNTIL SalesLines_L.NEXT=0
      ELSE
        ERROR(Text60008);

      IF Outsqty_L=0 THEN
        ERROR(Text60003)
      ELSE
        IF Outsqty_L<>Totqty_L THEN
          IF NOT CONFIRM(Text60004) THEN
            EXIT;

      CustDocDistribuiton_L.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
      CustDocDistribuiton_L.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
      IF NOT CustDocDistribuiton_L.FINDFIRST THEN
        ERROR(Text60005)
      ELSE BEGIN
        BlockedCustTxt_L:='';
        REPEAT
          CustRec_L.GET(CustDocDistribuiton_L."Distributed Customer No.");
          IF CustRec_L.Blocked <> CustRec_L.Blocked::" " THEN
            BlockedCustTxt_L+=STRSUBSTNO('\%1 - %2',CustRec_L."No.",CustRec_L.Name);


        UNTIL CustDocDistribuiton_L.NEXT=0;
        IF BlockedCustTxt_L<>'' THEN
          ERROR(Text60009,BlockedCustTxt_L);
      END;

      Distopt_L:=STRMENU(Text60006);
      CLEAR(CustDistMatrix_L);
      CASE Distopt_L OF
        1: CustDistMatrix_L.SetOpenPage(0);
        2: CustDistMatrix_L.SetOpenPage(1);
      ELSE
        EXIT
      END;

      CustDistMatrix_L.SETTABLEVIEW(SalesLines_L);
      CustDistMatrix_L.RUN;

      // <-- 20140903-APP21-TER01.01.00-PDC01
    END;

    PROCEDURE SplitSODistribution@60005();
    VAR
      CustDocDistribEntry_L@60000 : Record 60005;
      CustDocDistribEntryTemp_L@60013 : TEMPORARY Record 60005;
      NewSalesOrderHd_L@60003 : Record 36;
      OldSalesOrderHd_L@60014 : Record 36;
      FromSalesOrderLine_L@60005 : Record 37;
      NewSalesOrderLine_L@60004 : Record 37;
      FromSalesComments_L@60006 : Record 44;
      NewSalesComments_L@60007 : Record 44;
      SalesLines_L@60009 : Record 37;
      SalesLinesTemp_L@60012 : TEMPORARY Record 37;
      CustTemp_L@60002 : TEMPORARY Record 18;
      ArchiveManagement_L@60001 : Codeunit 5063;
      ReleaseSalesDoc_L@60011 : Codeunit 414;
      Outsqty_L@60008 : Decimal;
      Totqty_L@60010 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20140905-APP21-TER01.01.00-PDC01
      ArchiveManagement_L.StoreSalesDocument(Rec,FALSE);

      Outsqty_L:=0;
      Totqty_L:=0;
      SalesLines_L.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
      SalesLines_L.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
      SalesLines_L.SETRANGE(Type,SalesLines_L.Type::Item);
      IF SalesLines_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          Outsqty_L+=SalesLines_L."Outstanding Quantity";
          Totqty_L+=SalesLines_L.Quantity;
        UNTIL SalesLines_L.NEXT=0;

      CustTemp_L.DELETEALL;
      CustDocDistribEntry_L.RESET;
      CustDocDistribEntry_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Document Type","Document No.","Distributed Customer No.","Document Line No.");
      CustDocDistribEntry_L.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
      CustDocDistribEntry_L.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
      CustDocDistribEntry_L.SETFILTER("Distribution Qty.",'<>0');
      IF CustDocDistribEntry_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          IF ("Sell-to Customer No."<>CustDocDistribEntry_L."Distributed Customer No.") OR
             (Outsqty_L<>Totqty_L) THEN BEGIN
            CustTemp_L."No.":= CustDocDistribEntry_L."Distributed Customer No.";
            IF CustTemp_L.INSERT THEN;
          END;
        UNTIL CustDocDistribEntry_L.NEXT=0;

      FromSalesOrderLine_L.RESET;
      IF CustTemp_L.FINDFIRST THEN
        REPEAT
          CustDocDistribEntry_L.SETRANGE("Distributed Customer No.",CustTemp_L."No.");
          IF CustDocDistribEntry_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
            CLEAR(NewSalesOrderHd_L);
            NewSalesOrderHd_L.SetHideValidationDialog(TRUE);
            NewSalesOrderHd_L."Document Type":= "Document Type";
            // --> 20141112-APP21-TER01.01.00-31-FBE01
            //NewSalesOrderHd_L."Posting Date" := WORKDATE;
            //NewSalesOrderHd_L."Document Date" := "Document Date";
            // <-- 20141112-APP21-TER01.01.00-31-FBE01
            NewSalesOrderHd_L.INSERT(TRUE);
            NewSalesOrderHd_L.VALIDATE("Sell-to Customer No.",CustTemp_L."No.");
            //NewSalesOrderHd_L.VALIDATE("Bill-to Customer No.","Bill-to Customer No.");
            NewSalesOrderHd_L.VALIDATE("Bill-to Customer No.",CustTemp_L."No.");
            NewSalesOrderHd_L.VALIDATE("Shipment Date","Shipment Date");
            NewSalesOrderHd_L.VALIDATE("Requested Delivery Date","Requested Delivery Date");
            NewSalesOrderHd_L.VALIDATE("Promised Delivery Date","Promised Delivery Date");
            // --> 20141112-APP21-TER01.01.00-31-FBE01
            NewSalesOrderHd_L.VALIDATE("Posting Date", "Posting Date");
            NewSalesOrderHd_L.VALIDATE("Document Date", "Document Date");
            NewSalesOrderHd_L.VALIDATE("Order Date", "Order Date");
            // --> 20150126-APP21-TER01.01.00-152-SDE01
            NewSalesOrderHd_L.VALIDATE("Payment Terms Code","Payment Terms Code");
            NewSalesOrderHd_L.VALIDATE("Fixed Due Date","Fixed Due Date");
            NewSalesOrderHd_L.VALIDATE("Due Date","Due Date");
            // --> 20150203-APP21-TER01.01.00-SDE01
            NewSalesOrderHd_L."Assigned User ID":="Assigned User ID";
            // <-- 20150203-APP21-TER01.01.00-SDE01
            // <-- 20150126-APP21-TER01.01.00-152-SDE01
            // <-- 20141112-APP21-TER01.01.00-31-FBE01

            NewSalesOrderHd_L."Location Code":="Location Code";
            NewSalesOrderHd_L."Shipment Method Code":= "Shipment Method Code";
            NewSalesOrderHd_L."Shipping Agent Code" := "Shipping Agent Code";
            NewSalesOrderHd_L."Latest Req. Deliv. Date":="Latest Req. Deliv. Date";
            NewSalesOrderHd_L.Distributed:=TRUE;
            NewSalesOrderHd_L."Distributed SO No.":="No.";
            // --> 20150428-APP21-TER01.01.00-325-BSI01
            NewSalesOrderHd_L."SO Group Code" := "SO Group Code";
            // <-- 20150428-APP21-TER01.01.00-325-BSI01

            // --> 20151120-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2447-CJY01
            NewSalesOrderHd_L.SetGroupAccount;
            // <-- 20151120-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2447-CJY01
            // --> 20171023-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26615-CJY01
            NewSalesOrderHd_L."Document Source" := "Document Source";
            // <-- 20171023-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26615-CJY01
            // --> 20181001-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3353-CJY01
            NewSalesOrderHd_L."External Document No." := "External Document No.";
            NewSalesOrderHd_L."Format Code Isagri CRM Guid" := "Format Code Isagri CRM Guid";
            // <-- 20181001-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3353-CJY01

            FromSalesComments_L.SETRANGE("No.","No.");

                NewSalesComments_L."No.":=NewSalesOrderHd_L."No.";

              UNTIL FromSalesComments_L.NEXT=0;

              NewSalesOrderLine_L.INSERT(TRUE);
              NewSalesOrderLine_L.DistributionProcessRunning(TRUE);
              NewSalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE(Type,NewSalesOrderLine_L.Type::Item);

              NewSalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE("No.",FromSalesOrderLine_L."No.");
              // --> 20171218-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
              NewSalesOrderLine_L.SuspendStatusCheck(TRUE);
              // <-- 20171218-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01

              IF NewSalesOrderLine_L."Build Kit" THEN BEGIN
                NewSalesOrderLine_L.SuspendStatusCheck(FALSE);
                NewSalesOrderLine_L.AutoValidBuildKitOnLineChange(7);
              END
              ELSE BEGIN
              // <-- 20171218-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
              NewSalesOrderLine_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
              // --> 20171218-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
              END;
              // <-- 20171218-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01

              // --> 20150731-APP21-ADV01.01.00-1995-FEC01
              CLEAR(TransferExtendedText);
              IF TransferExtendedText.SalesCheckIfAnyExtText(NewSalesOrderLine_L,TRUE) THEN BEGIN
                TransferExtendedText.SetSkipCommit(TRUE);
                TransferExtendedText.InsertSalesExtText(NewSalesOrderLine_L);
                TransferExtendedText.SetSkipCommit(FALSE);
              END;
              // <-- 20150731-APP21-ADV01.01.00-1995-FEC01

              FromSalesComments_L.SETRANGE("Document Line No.",CustDocDistribEntry_L."Document Line No.");
              IF FromSalesComments_L.FINDSET THEN
                REPEAT
                  NewSalesComments_L:=FromSalesComments_L;
                  NewSalesComments_L."No.":=NewSalesOrderHd_L."No.";
                  NewSalesComments_L.INSERT;
                UNTIL FromSalesComments_L.NEXT=0;

            UNTIL CustDocDistribEntry_L.NEXT=0;

            CLEAR(ReleaseSalesDoc_L);
            ReleaseSalesDoc_L.RUN(NewSalesOrderHd_L);

          END;
        UNTIL CustTemp_L.NEXT=0;

      CLEAR(ReleaseSalesDoc_L);
      ReleaseSalesDoc_L.Reopen(Rec);
      CustDocDistribEntry_L.SETRANGE("Distributed Customer No.","Sell-to Customer No.");
      IF (CustDocDistribEntry_L.FINDFIRST) AND (Outsqty_L=Totqty_L) THEN BEGIN

        SalesLinesTemp_L.DELETEALL;
        CustDocDistribEntryTemp_L.DELETEALL;
        REPEAT
          CustDocDistribEntryTemp_L:=CustDocDistribEntry_L;
          CustDocDistribEntryTemp_L.INSERT;
        UNTIL CustDocDistribEntry_L.NEXT=0;

        FromSalesOrderLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
        FromSalesOrderLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
        FromSalesOrderLine_L.SETRANGE(Type,FromSalesOrderLine_L.Type::Item);
        IF FromSalesOrderLine_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            SalesLinesTemp_L:=FromSalesOrderLine_L;
            SalesLinesTemp_L.INSERT;
          UNTIL FromSalesOrderLine_L.NEXT=0;

        FromSalesOrderLine_L.SETRANGE(Type);
        FromSalesOrderLine_L.DELETEALL(TRUE);

        HideValidationDialog:=TRUE;
        //--> 20150107-APP21-TER01.01.00-PDC01
        OldSalesOrderHd_L:=Rec;
        //<-- 20150107-APP21-TER01.01.00-PDC01
        VALIDATE("Sell-to Customer No.","Sell-to Customer No.");
        // --> 20150331-APP21-TER01.01.00-291-FBE01
        VALIDATE("Bill-to Customer No.","Sell-to Customer No.");
        MODIFY;
        // <-- 20150331-APP21-TER01.01.00-291-FBE01
        IF CustDocDistribEntryTemp_L.FINDFIRST THEN
          REPEAT

            SalesLinesTemp_L.GET(CustDocDistribEntryTemp_L."Document Type",CustDocDistribEntryTemp_L."Document No.",
                                 CustDocDistribEntryTemp_L."Document Line No.");

            NewSalesOrderLine_L.INIT;
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Document Type":="Document Type";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Document No.":="No.";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Sell-to Customer No.":="Sell-to Customer No.";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Bill-to Customer No.":="Sell-to Customer No.";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Line No.":= NewSalesOrderLine_L.GetLastLine + 10000;
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.INSERT(TRUE);
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.DistributionProcessRunning(TRUE);
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE(Type,NewSalesOrderLine_L.Type::Item);
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE("No.",SalesLinesTemp_L."No.");
            // --> 20171218-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
            IF NewSalesOrderLine_L."Build Kit" THEN
                NewSalesOrderLine_L.SuspendStatusCheck(TRUE);
            // <-- 20171218-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE("Variant Code",SalesLinesTemp_L."Variant Code");
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE("Location Code",SalesLinesTemp_L."Location Code");
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE("Unit of Measure Code",SalesLinesTemp_L."Unit of Measure Code");
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.Distributed:=TRUE;  // it is necessary to be here
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE(Quantity,CustDocDistribEntryTemp_L."Distribution Qty.");
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE("Shipment Date",SalesLinesTemp_L."Shipment Date");
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE("Requested Delivery Date",SalesLinesTemp_L."Requested Delivery Date");
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE("Promised Delivery Date",SalesLinesTemp_L."Promised Delivery Date");
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE("Unit Price",SalesLinesTemp_L."Unit Price");
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE("Line Discount %",SalesLinesTemp_L."Line Discount %");
            //--> 20150107-APP21-TER01.01.00-PDC01
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE("Net Unit Price",SalesLinesTemp_L."Net Unit Price");
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Latest Req. Deliv. Date":= SalesLinesTemp_L."Latest Req. Deliv. Date";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."ECD Unit Amount" := SalesLinesTemp_L."ECD Unit Amount";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Budgeted Unit Price":=SalesLinesTemp_L."Budgeted Unit Price";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Budgeted Unit Price (Sales)":=SalesLinesTemp_L."Budgeted Unit Price (Sales)";
            // --> 20170518-WI00-ADV01.01.00-27240-CJY01
            {BEFORE
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."ECD Calculation Priority":=SalesLinesTemp_L."ECD Calculation Priority"::"Budgeted Unit Price";
            AFTER}
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."ECD Calculation Priority":=SalesLinesTemp_L."ECD Calculation Priority";
            // <-- 20170518-WI00-ADV01.01.00-27240-CJY01
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Customer Price Group":=SalesLinesTemp_L."Customer Price Group";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Customer Disc. Group":=SalesLinesTemp_L."Customer Disc. Group";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Shipment Method Code":=SalesLinesTemp_L."Shipment Method Code";
            //<-- 20150107-APP21-TER01.01.00-PDC01
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Negotiated Price":=SalesLinesTemp_L."Negotiated Price";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Market Price":=SalesLinesTemp_L."Market Price";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Season ID No.":=SalesLinesTemp_L."Season ID No.";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."CMC Campaign No.":=SalesLinesTemp_L."CMC Campaign No.";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.Distributed:=TRUE;
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Distributed SO No.":="No.";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Third Grouping":=SalesLinesTemp_L."Third Grouping";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Quantity before delivery":=SalesLinesTemp_L."Outstanding Quantity";
            // --> 20150203-APP21-TER01.01.00-152-SDE01
            //NewSalesOrderLine_L."Due Date":=NewSalesOrderHd_L."Due Date";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE("Payment Terms Code",FromSalesOrderLine_L."Payment Terms Code");
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE("Due Date",FromSalesOrderLine_L."Due Date");
            // <-- 20150203-APP21-TER01.01.00-152-SDE01
            // --> 20150903-APP19-ADV01.01.00-1901-FBE01
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Kit BOM No." := FromSalesOrderLine_L."Kit BOM No.";
            // <-- 20150903-APP19-ADV01.01.00-1901-FBE01
            // --> 20160315-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-2844-JCD01
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Location Code Prod. Mix" := FromSalesOrderLine_L."Location Code Prod. Mix";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Item Season Code" := FromSalesOrderLine_L."Item Season Code";
            NewSalesOrderLine_L."Salesperson Code" := FromSalesOrderLine_L."Salesperson Code";
            // <-- 20160315-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-2844-JCD01
            // --> 20171218-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
            IF NewSalesOrderLine_L."Build Kit" THEN BEGIN
              NewSalesOrderLine_L.SuspendStatusCheck(FALSE);
              NewSalesOrderLine_L.AutoValidBuildKitOnLineChange(7);
            END
            ELSE BEGIN
            // <-- 20171218-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
            NewSalesOrderLine_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
            // --> 20171218-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
            END;
            // <-- 20171218-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
            // --> 20150731-APP21-ADV01.01.00-1995-FEC02
            CLEAR(TransferExtendedText);
            IF TransferExtendedText.SalesCheckIfAnyExtText(NewSalesOrderLine_L,TRUE) THEN BEGIN
              TransferExtendedText.SetSkipCommit(TRUE);
              TransferExtendedText.InsertSalesExtText(NewSalesOrderLine_L);
              TransferExtendedText.SetSkipCommit(FALSE);
            END;
            // <-- 20150731-APP21-ADV01.01.00-1995-FEC02

          UNTIL CustDocDistribEntryTemp_L.NEXT=0;

        "To Distribute":=FALSE;
        Distributed:=TRUE;
        "Distributed SO No.":="No.";

        //--> 20150107-APP21-TER01.01.00-PDC01

      // <-- 20140905-APP21-TER01.01.00-PDC01
    END;

    PROCEDURE RunShptDistribution@1000000003();
    VAR
      SalesShptLines_L@60000 : Record 111;
      CustDocDistribution_L@60003 : Record 60004;
      CustRec_L@60007 : Record 18;
      CustDistMatrix_L@60004 : Page 60084;
      CreditLimitWarning_L@60008 : Page 343;
      Outsqty_L@60001 : Decimal;
      Totqty_L@60002 : Decimal;
      Distopt_L@60005 : Integer;
      BlockedCustTxt_L@60006 : Text[1024];
      SalesLine_L@1000000000 : Record 37;
      FrmCustDistMatrix_L@1000000001 : Form 60084;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20141209-APP21-TER01.01.00-FEC01
      TESTFIELD(Status,Status::Released);

      IF NOT "To Distribute" THEN
        ERROR(Text60001);

      SalesLine_L.RESET;
      SalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
      SalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
      SalesLine_L.SETFILTER("Quantity Shipped",'<>0');
      IF SalesLine_L.ISEMPTY THEN
        ERROR(Text60013);
      // --> 20141231-APP21-TER01.01.00-SDE01
      {
      SalesLine_L.RESET;
      SalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
      SalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
      SalesLine_L.SETFILTER("Quantity Invoiced",'<>0');
      IF NOT SalesLine_L.ISEMPTY THEN
        ERROR(Text60012);
      }
      // <-- 20141231-APP21-TER01.01.00-SDE01

      IF ("Payment Discount %"<>0) OR
         ("Prepayment %"<>0) OR
         ("Prepmt. Payment Discount %"<>0) THEN
        ERROR(Text60002);

      Outsqty_L:=0;
      Totqty_L:=0;
      SalesShptLines_L.SETRANGE("Order No.","No.");
      SalesShptLines_L.SETRANGE(Type,SalesShptLines_L.Type::Item);
      SalesShptLines_L.SETFILTER(Quantity,'>0');
      SalesShptLines_L.SETFILTER("Qty. Shipped Not Invoiced",'>0');
      IF SalesShptLines_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          Outsqty_L+=SalesShptLines_L."Qty. Shipped Not Invoiced";
          Totqty_L+=SalesShptLines_L.Quantity;
        UNTIL SalesShptLines_L.NEXT=0
      ELSE
        ERROR(Text60008);

      IF Outsqty_L=0 THEN
        ERROR(Text60003)
      ELSE
        IF Outsqty_L<>Totqty_L THEN
          IF NOT CONFIRM(Text60004) THEN
            EXIT;

      CustDocDistribution_L.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
      CustDocDistribution_L.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
      IF NOT CustDocDistribution_L.FINDFIRST THEN
        ERROR(Text60005)
      ELSE BEGIN
        BlockedCustTxt_L:='';
        REPEAT
          CustRec_L.GET(CustDocDistribution_L."Distributed Customer No.");
          IF CustRec_L.Blocked <> CustRec_L.Blocked::" " THEN
            BlockedCustTxt_L+=STRSUBSTNO('\%1 - %2',CustRec_L."No.",CustRec_L.Name);
        UNTIL CustDocDistribution_L.NEXT=0;
        IF BlockedCustTxt_L<>'' THEN
          ERROR(Text60009,BlockedCustTxt_L);
      END;

      Distopt_L:=STRMENU(Text60006);

      // --> 20160620-APP21-TER01.01.00-2636-OAN02
      // BEFORE
      {CLEAR(CustDistMatrix_L);
      CASE Distopt_L OF
        1: CustDistMatrix_L.SetOpenPage(0);
        2: CustDistMatrix_L.SetOpenPage(1);
      ELSE
        EXIT
      END;
      CustDistMatrix_L.SETTABLEVIEW(SalesShptLines_L);
      CustDistMatrix_L.RUN;}
      // AFTER
      IF ISSERVICETIER THEN BEGIN
        CLEAR(CustDistMatrix_L);
        CASE Distopt_L OF
          1: CustDistMatrix_L.SetOpenPage(0);
          2: CustDistMatrix_L.SetOpenPage(1);
        ELSE
          EXIT
        END;
        CustDistMatrix_L.SETTABLEVIEW(SalesShptLines_L);
        CustDistMatrix_L.RUN;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        CLEAR(FrmCustDistMatrix_L);
        CASE Distopt_L OF
          1: FrmCustDistMatrix_L.SetOpenPage(0);
          2: FrmCustDistMatrix_L.SetOpenPage(1);
        ELSE
          EXIT
        END;
        FrmCustDistMatrix_L.SETTABLEVIEW(SalesShptLines_L);
        FrmCustDistMatrix_L.RUN;
      END;
      // <-- 20141209-APP21-TER01.01.00-FEC01
    END;

    PROCEDURE FillInSalesOrderUsedNo@60006(RecSalesHead_P@60001 : Record 36);
    VAR
      RecSalesOrderUsedNo_L@60000 : Record 60056;
      RecSalesLine_L@60002 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20150507-APP45-TER01.01.00-112-BSI01
      IF RecSalesHead_P."Document Type" <> RecSalesHead_P."Document Type"::Order THEN
        EXIT;

      // --> 20150528-APP45-TER01.01.00-112-BSI01
      CLEAR(RecSalesLine_L);
      RecSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",RecSalesHead_P."Document Type");
      RecSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",RecSalesHead_P."No.");
      RecSalesLine_L.SETRANGE(Type,RecSalesLine_L.Type::Item);
      IF RecSalesLine_L.FINDFIRST THEN;
      // <-- 20150528-APP45-TER01.01.00-112-BSI01


      RecSalesOrderUsedNo_L.INIT;
      RecSalesOrderUsedNo_L."Order No." := RecSalesHead_P."No.";
      RecSalesOrderUsedNo_L."Document Date" := RecSalesHead_P."Document Date";
      RecSalesOrderUsedNo_L."Salesperson Code" := RecSalesLine_L."Salesperson Code";
      RecSalesOrderUsedNo_L."External Reference No." := RecSalesHead_P."External Reference No.";
      IF NOT RecSalesOrderUsedNo_L.INSERT THEN
        RecSalesOrderUsedNo_L.MODIFY;

      // <-- 20150507-APP45-TER01.01.00-112-BSI01
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteFromSalesOrderUsedNo@60007(RecSalesHead_P@60001 : Record 36);
    VAR
      RecSalesOrderUsedNo_L@60000 : Record 60056;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20150507-APP45-TER01.01.00-112-BSI01
      IF RecSalesHead_P."Document Type" <> RecSalesHead_P."Document Type"::Order THEN
        EXIT;

      IF RecSalesOrderUsedNo_L.GET(RecSalesHead_P."No.") THEN
        RecSalesOrderUsedNo_L.DELETE;
      // <-- 20150507-APP45-TER01.01.00-112-BSI01
    END;

    PROCEDURE CampainNoLookUp@60008();
    VAR
      CMCSetup_L@60000 : Record 8052982;
      RecCampaign_L@60001 : Record 8052991;
      PageCampains_L@60002 : Page 8052994;
    BEGIN
      CLEAR(RecCampaign_L);
      CMCSetup_L.GET;
      IF CMCSetup_L."Display Only Open & Rele. Camp" THEN BEGIN
        RecCampaign_L.FILTERGROUP(2);
        RecCampaign_L.SETFILTER(Status,'<>%1',RecCampaign_L.Status::Closed);
        RecCampaign_L.FILTERGROUP(0);
      END;

      PageCampains_L.SETTABLEVIEW(RecCampaign_L);
      PageCampains_L.LOOKUPMODE := TRUE;
      IF PageCampains_L.RUNMODAL = ACTION::LookupOK THEN BEGIN
        PageCampains_L.GETRECORD(RecCampaign_L);
        VALIDATE("CMC Campaign No.",RecCampaign_L."No.");
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateShiptoCountryRegionCode@1000000009();

      PostCodeRec_L@1000000000 : Record 225;

      END;
      // <-- 20260409-WI00-ADV01.01.00-43405-SAN01
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckSendToAP@1000000010();
    VAR
      Contact_L@1000000000 : Record 5050;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20260224-WI00-ADV01.01.00-42635-IJI
      IF ("Document Type" <> "Document Type"::Invoice) AND ("Document Type" <>"Document Type"::"Credit Memo") THEN
        EXIT;

      IF (xRec."Bill-to Customer No." = "Bill-to Customer No.") THEN
        EXIT;

      // --> 20260528-WI00-ADV01.01.00-42635-CJY
      "Send to Approved Platform" := "Send to Approved Platform"::Yes
      {
      IF Contact_L.GET("Bill-to Customer No.") THEN
        IF (Contact_L."Electronic Invoice" = Contact_L."Electronic Invoice"::" ") THEN
          "Send to Approved Platform" := "Send to Approved Platform"::Yes
        ELSE
          "Send to Approved Platform" := "Send to Approved Platform"::No;
      }
      // <-- 20260224-WI00-ADV01.01.00-42635-IJI
      // <-- 20260528-WI00-ADV01.01.00-42635-CJY
    END;
